- RsbR is involved in environmental stress response
- RsbU is a phosphatase involved in environmental stress
- RsbP is involved in energy stress (carbon starvation)

RsbP is a PP2C-type phosphatase that:
- Acts as a positive regulator of sigma-B activity (by dephosphorylating RsbV, allowing RsbV to sequester RsbW and release sigma-B)
- Specifically responds to energy stress
- It&#x27;s a phosphatase, so it DEphosphorylates RsbV

RsbP in Bacillus subtilis:

- This allows RsbV to bind RsbW, releasing sigma-B
- Therefore it&#x27;s a POSITIVE regulator of sigma-B activity

Looking at the options:
